## Prologue

Yawning Portal – dungeon inside, looking to find treasure within.

Party knows each other from previous work together – ridding the area of ‘bandits’

---
## Session 1

Inside the tavern, a large 40’ diameter well in the middle of the loud and boisterous common area. Large, burly barkeep (DURNAN) is passing out drinks to the lively patrons.

Approach the bar – begin drinking a flagon of ale. He says most people come looking to be lowered into the dungeon. 1 GP to gain access/person. Bromnik in search of magical smithy tools for crafting magical weapon/armors.

Woman approaches the group – asks for magic items, offering to pay a price, if they make it out alive.

Party tosses their coins to the barkeep – to the depths they go! THE BAR BEGINS BETTING ON HOW LONG THEY WILL LAST…

Group lands on the ground floor, approx. 40’ diameter room, sandy bottom, pitch black. George gives ‘light’ – making it visible around the group. Clip Clop beings to notice the red ‘writing’, elvish – through these mad halls the mage awaits, casting spells behind magic gates… writing has been there for a long time. Bucket to go up – 2g to go back up/person.

Being moving down the hallway, opens up into a larger cavernous hallway – with statues. Bromnik tosses pebbles at the base of the statue, no effect. George attempts to detect magic – no effect. Clop runs down the hall, identifies chair with skeleton pointing at statue. George swipes the femur (Kenku). Investigate statues further, no other information is gained. Walking into a large chamber with pillars, approx. 50’ tall. Clop neighs into the void – no response. Smell of musty and decay. Bromnik casts Blessing of the Forge on Holden’s Greatsword - +1 to attack/dmg. Bromnik stealth’s towards a shadow, humanoid, slowly writhing on his stomach, moving slowly. George casts light on his dagger in order to see, something looks back from the darkness, small bug type creature the size of a large dog, with tentacles – screams in our direction. Another is crawling from behind the pillar, and another slithering down from the ceiling. x3 total = FIGHT. Tentacles + poison possible. KILL! George loots femur x1, Clip Clop loots tentacles x4 – but gets hit by a barb, gets poisoned…Bromnik has to suck out the poison, FFS.

Right Door, no trap, enter. Find armor stand, glowing sword. George does arcana check – glowing and lightening up the room, yellowish/white. Hand on the ground, half-orc. Clip Clop takes the sword – magic, taking to sell it – IT IS BOUND to Clip Clop. Arcana check – CURSED sword. Stuck in his hand, dispel magic has no effect. Find small gem under rubble, Holden picks it up – purple gem.

--REMOVE CURSE – learn at long rest, to remove item

Continue down long hallway – going left at the T intersection, opening up into a bigger space. Dimly lit – can see much, pile of bones potentially.

FIGHT – Bird Brain x2, large brain with beak, screeching, and tentacles. (12? AC) Clip Clop is paralyzed and bound, Bromnik revives him and heals, he breaks free. Bromnik is grappled, no paralyzed – didn’t break free. Holden strikes down beast 1 while grappled, Bromnik word of radiance on remaining. Eldrich blasts – BOOM right the stupid fucking beakface, exploding it.

## Session 2

Short Rest – roll x3 hit dice.

Investigate current room – wardrobe appears off, has stained ‘red’ liquid and some hole in the back of it. Bromnik pries open the wardrobe with a piece of the wood from the pile of crates. Clip clop and Bromnik flip the wardrobe open – a small body falls out (child size?), only a few days old – it is a gnome with dark grey skin, definitely dead, front of tunic is saturated with blood wearing a satchel. George investigates and searches pockets. Bromnik medicine checks wounds – stab wound to the gut, leading to massive blood loss. Piece of paper with a symbol (Moon with a harp inside) on it inside breast pocket, and a lockbox in satchel, with some small rations.

George knows of the symbol – organization is ‘the harper’s’ – secret in origin = equivalent of SHIELD in marvel. They deal with evil doers, securing magical items back from the baddies. The letter is opened, but blank – possible invisible ink. Clip pierces the letter with magic sword that is glowing, revealing the letter’s words:

_Simon deliver this lockbox to our contacts in skull port, be careful – there is payment upon delivery_

Skull port is a place of ill repute, smugglers bay type location – of which it is at the entrance of a mountain? True to their word mercenaries, will likely pay. George grabs the satchel with the lockbox, will hold onto it for now. Group begins to move down long hall, reaching a doorway. Inside it is a large room, dimly lit light in the far back of the room, there is a statue in the middle of it – dirt in front of it, shadows exist. Sneak inside using stealth, perception check – candles appear to be in the back, shadows of cloth tarps or tents. Clip Clop begins to sneak with Bromnik – but does it loudly. A human-like figure comes from behind the statue, wearing a long black cloak, pale skin, only his eyes are visible, with white hair and ornate jewelry, fangs in his mouth exposed – “I want to suck your blood, I am a creature of the night” – VAMPIRE. Insight check – guy is acting _weird_…but not likely a vampire. Bromnik hails him in a friendly manner – but is told the party is in a stick up. Renaissance style clothing on the group, but worn, they are armed and staring at the group.

George INTIMIDATES the group, they agree to not attack, in exchange for help. Hobgoblins, goblin types have been spying on ‘us’, preventing anyone from going into the lower levels. They have many secrets, help us dispatch them. George beings to talk down to them, George intimidates while slapping his cock in his hands like a band, menacingly. Clip clop grabs the map with his dick from the lead of the group – hastily written, not significant detail- revealing outpost throughout the dungeon, where hobgoblins are likely stationed. The troop has been down here for months…it seems. Clip Clop attempts to rid himself of the sword with the leader of the party, after receiving 2g, but it magically re-appears in Clip Clop’s hands. Go through North door, down hallway, choose LEFT path. Continue down another long hallway, ending at a T intersection, left shows two unmarked rooms and right shows another long hallway ending in double doors. Group goes to the left path, there is a door and hallway keeps going ending at a door. Open door in wall, hundreds and hundreds of skulls pour out of the door! Clip Clop grabs Bromnik with his horse cock, pulling them both to safety. The skulls are of various creatures and sizes. Go to the end of the hall and open the door – peering in with light sword. There is ‘something’ against the back wall. It appears to be a nice condition throne – on the seat itself is a helmet (spartan style). History check in throne, made by the humans by the style. Bromnik uses Identify ritual on the helm – not realllllly a magical item, but it does have magic. It reveals _something,_ not sure what though – placing it in Bromnik’s pack to later use. Begin back down the hallway, 100+ feet, with alcoves along the walls. On either side in the alcoves are mirrors…able to see reflections. George and Bromnik avert their eyes, but Clip Clop flexes and admires himself – but sees a different image, a woman at a desk looking up at Clip. She is in ethereal form, moving towards the group, wearing fine cloth. Has a face of concern, places her hand on the mirror, and it turns back to a normal mirror. End of hallway has LARGE double doors, halfway through there is a smaller door in the left side. Two dead spiders in middle of room. Shadowy figures appear out of the mirror, looking exactly like George and Bromnik – BATTLE!

Does NOT take necrotic damage…but Holden was able to strike through it. George eldritch blasts Ghost George, poof – they disappear fully. Mirrors are still glowing – George attempts to break the mirror with a dagger throw, creating a crack in it. Eldritch blasts the mirror, shatters it fully, turning off the magic – do so with the rest of the mirrors to make it ‘safe’. Investigate the door, it appears to be ‘off’. There are runes/magic wards placed around the door. History check on the runes, high level magic. George casts spell to know any language.

George blasts the door open, party goes through it. Down the hall, to the right, large chamber. Perceive a large statue holding something. Being to slowly move into the room, looking at the roof it appears domed, and there may be something on it? Floating at the top of the room, water like in nature, shimmering approximately 30’ up. Clip Clop moves to inspect the statue, it looks like a large fishman, holding something in its grasp – looks to be a small treasure chest, heart shape lock hole. George spider legs it up to the shimmering thing, its green and creepy – it is ACID attack. George takes 9 acid damage on his hand. Clip Clop attempts to lockpick the chest on the statue – success, magical force emanates, causing acid to fall from the ceiling – attempting to hide underneath it. Clip Clop looks into the chest (scroll – magical, scroll of invisibility, key – large, iron, shriveled heart – George). Clip Clop somehow manages to monk his ass out of the room without taking damage.

## Session 3

Back in hall of broken mirrors, Clip Clop attempts to knock the door down with STR – no luck. Attempt to open the door with iron key – it begins to open, key glowing brightly like the ruins…and disappears! Ruins begin to flash, brighter, and all lighting up – forming a symbol on the door, green. The Symbol burst through Clip like a flashbang – confusing him, making him unaware of what is going on around him and what he was doing – beginning to do kung fu moves uncontrollably. He ends his fighting, regaining normalcy.

George peers into the very large room, smelling something – but unknown. Dark room with statue in middle of the room, potentially, along with stacks and piles of stuff, giving off an odor of decay and refuse. George throws daggers with light into the room, left and right – 30’, hitting the wall and falling to the ground. A large creature from the right side of the room comes barreling over, body of a lion/face of a mangled human with jagged teeth/mane of bristles in hair down its back/with a spiky tail. It moves towards the knife and inspects with growls, with bat wings.

The creature charges the door, knocking it wide open – in front of the group. Shoots barbs from tail at George, 4hp, impaling him. Clip charges at him – hitting for 9hp (Ki). Hit x2 is 4hp = 13hp. Holden attacks for 13hp, miss. Bromnik casts sacred flame – 9hp hit. George sends eldritch blasts – hitting for 23hp. Clip clop attempts to front flip kick him (x2 critical fails…) lands under the beast and prone. TWO MORE SNARL AND COME TOWARDS THE GROUP. Clip Clop moves to attack again after getting up from the ground after failed parkour moves…Holden attacks – KILLING the beast.

Bromnik casts guiding bolt – ZAPPPPP 23HP from near beast. George curses the beast, disadvantage on STR saving. Beast 2 flies up and shoots barbs and George and Bromnik – hitting both! 8/5 damage. Clip clop gets shot by beast 1 -x2 barbs = hitting – taking huge damage…attempts to deflect missile, taking 12dmg and deflecting 8! Clip Clop – CRIT ON ADV! Clip Clop throws the spear back at the beast, impaling it in the chest for 15dmg, lowering itself to 10ft off the ground. He moves to uppercut the beast, falls. Bromnik attempts to shoot with crossbow bolt – misses. George eldritch blasts – ZAP for 18dmg. Clip is nearly pinned, but takes 7dmg. Beast 2 flies towards Bromnik – misses on one, but takes 10dmg on bolt 2 to the GROIN. Holden runs in and breathes fire on the flying beast – causing it to crash to the ground in flames taking 11 dmg, burning its wings and preventing flight. Clip Clop attempts to strike him, but misses. Holden moves to attack the burned one – hitting him for 11 dmg + 13dmg?.  Bromnik cast AID on team, and healed Clip Clop for 10. George attempts to blast, but misses both bolts. Holden is attacked and hit twice for 15 dmg. Clip Clop is attacked for 7 dmg… Clip Clop moves to attack, cutting off its testicles – dropping dead from pain. He rushes behind the other beast and attacks for 7 damage. Holden moves to cleave again hitting it. Bromnik attempts toll the dead, no dice…FFS. George Eldritch Blasts the beast in the face and ass, zapping him into death. George harvest a quill (javelin size). Clip Clop removes manticore pelts, skinning them, receiving x4. George harvests x2 eyes.

Investigate room, finding: Flail, small wooden flute, chain shirt, small pouch – jingles with 14S/21G, large pendant made with red blood stone.

Investigate lab/alchemy alcove – finding vials with debris floating in them, some clear vials. Open vial, quick waft, and close to test. Compound of alchemy, unknown…poison kit, perhaps. Investigate the statue – old stone…

Prep the room, bar the doors, place jars on the wood jams to early warning alert, poison tentacles hanging from the door, continue to prep, make small makeshift campfire – ahead of long rest!

George’s tome appears, and the pages appear to have the beast he has killed. It speaks to him, ‘bring me more, show me more despair’.

## Session 4

After long rest –

Bromnik begins to know details about the stonework in the dungeon, definitely dwarven, there is historical context for it – can’t recall specifics, but further journey may job memory. Eaten breakfast – manticore bacon!

Begin moving into the next room, there is a lizard/alligator body on the ground – Clip whips with tentacle…it appears dead. A large insect creature burst from within it, several large size come from inside – large snake size. They move to attack Clip – Bromnik moves to attack the right one, for 7 dmg, smashing it with his Warhammer. George investigates the lizard creature – it is a basilisk! Has the ability to turn people into stone. Perception check – no other sound or movement in the next room, it seems. There is a crystal sphere in the claws of the creature, George hands it to Bromnik to identify.

It is a drift globe, Clip takes it and has control of it (controllable light source within 60ft). Clip shines it around the room, investigating – identifying more statues. Religion check on the dark statue, it is the GOD OF INVENTION – GOND. George dispels magic on the black onyx statue, a pool of black tar moves towards George. It moves to attack George, missing. Bromnik jumps backwards. Holden breathes fire on it – Bromnik misses, Clip tosses hammer from pack into it – the ooze destroys it. George blasts it with eldritch – hitting for big damage. Holden AC takes -1 due to acidic and eroding mechanic to armor. Bromnik casts Sacred Flame for 8dmg. Clip attempts to wall run and stab it with the cursed weapon – 8dmg. It causes the ooze beast to split into two separate beings. George zaps the smaller one for 18dmg, causing it to seep into the ground – dead, one remains. Holden attempts to throw a statue at the blob, but it falls to the ground broken. Bromnik heals Clip for +9hp, and scared flame damage for 12hp on the ooze blob. Clip dashes away using step of the wind and disengage. Clip tosses a statue at it for bludgeoning damage, doing 6 damage – swallowing the statue. Eldritch BLAST for 22 damage – looking weak. Bromnik casts toll the dead, exploding it into droplets of ooze across the back wall. Cast heal of prayer, healing 18hp for all party.

Clip and party move back to door and pass the orb down the hall, Bromnik goes first….regular hallway for a good distance until begin to hear whispers all around the group. Aberations move towards the group quickly from behind – running past the group. Appears to play out a scene of a beast chasing the party. Continuing on the path it begins to go lower and to the right, there are bright flashes along the wall – accompanied by a faint noise, slight buzzing. Group continues onwards, attempting to stealth closer. George peaks around the corner to get a closer look, seeing two large pillars that are decaying and crumbling with debris on the ground. Stonecunning on the arch – determine its not dwarven, not a ton due to its state, seems to be of magical origin. Clip picks up a bone from the skeleton into the ‘gateway’ – goes through without causing an electrical strike. George arcana check – is of magic, appears to be malfunctioning.

Bromnik has been writing on the map this entire time, making note of locations, it appears there is an outpost where goblins is immediately up ahead.

## Session 5

After short rest, begin moving down the hall – Clip is moving first in order with drift globe ~5ft ahead. Group approaches a T intersection – move to investigate the room with no door immediately front. Inside are large human sized cages, empty, but dried blood on the floor and cage doors with bones and some decayed remains. The sound of faint voices is heard from down the long hall to the right. Move into the cage room and investigate the cage room – hearing a goblin voice saying ‘they are coming, ready!’. Holden picks up Bromnik and throws at the door like cannonball – shield first, taking bludgeoning damage (2). George zaps one, Clip stabs archer with glowing dagger, Bromnik word of radiance on the spearman – exploding in a ball of light, Holden stabs the spearman in the face. More goblins rush into the room! Another creature comes forward – BUGBEAR – he yells we do not belong here and to BE GONE – moving to attack Clip Clop for 12dmg. A patrol is coming from down the long hall…PATROL! George casts darkness behind the group, 15’ radius. Clip casts necrotic damage with magic punch (15dmg). Bromnik casts 9 fire damage on bugbear with heat metal and casts healing word to heal for 10hp on CLIP. Bugbear weathers heat metal, but misses clip when attacking. Another bugbear and goblin appear from the rear through the darkness, throwing a spear at George. Holden executes the bugbear, cleaving his head completely off. Bromnik casts toll the dead – necrotic damage withers the goblin to death immediately. Bugbear charges in towards Bromnik – missing and glancing off the shield. Archer goblin fires a bolt at Bromnik, causing 12dmg. Bromnik toll the dead on bugbear, exploding his head – a perfectly intact brain plops to the ground. It gets legs and begins to move, and an additional brain breaches from the head Holden cut off. Opportunity attack – HIT with Warhammer for 7dmg and Clip punches for 9dmg, squishing it – but it flees away through the darkness. George slams the other with a book that he throws – causing slime, but it flees. More goblins rush in to attack Clip, but miss. Bromnik is hit with another arrow. George attempts to flee after the brain, stabbing it for a moment with a dagger. Clip round house kicks the goblin, smashing him into the wall – killing it. Clip JCVD kicks the other goblin to the other wall, killing him. Clip lou kang kicks the last goblin into an open cage, closing the door as it slides down in death.

Brain chase! Bromnik moves to George to try and smother the brain with a blanket – grappled!

Brain 2 speaks to Clip, sounding like a child, asking to be let go. Clip lasso it with tentacle, latches on – but brain attacks and hits for 8 slashing dmg – and attempts to get into its head, but able to shrug it off. Brain 1 attempts to break free from the blanket – unable to break free.  Holden slashes at the caught brain, despite the team trying to capture it, for 7dmg + 10dmg. George changes his mind and moves to attack with eldritch blasts – killing it, and destroying Bromnik’s blanket in the process. Clip lashes out with another tentacle, moving to hogtie/grapple it. Brain2 mind attacks Clip – causing massive damage, 13 psychic damage, and worst headache – ever. INTELLIGENCE score drops to 0, stunning him into immobility. Bromnik rushes over to help Clip – guiding bolt to Brain 2, bursting into light and death. Clip is stunned – can not perform any function. Medicine check reveals nothing, arcana check did psychic damage to his brain. Begin to try to hunker down for a long rest…LONG REST in bedroom. Cast Remove Curse on Clip – sword curse is removed, regular sword that glows – strap it to Bromnik’s pack. Bromnik creates a metal wheelchair for Clip using Artisan’s Blessing – channel divinity, creating spikey wheels with darts and a way to have the sword act as a joust with tentacles.

Investigate room(s):

Stockpile of random stuff, weapons, shields, armor, adventuring gear, etc.

Chest filled with coin: 500S / 10G = ~60G

## Session 6

Blessing of the forge - +1AC to Holden – attempt to pray to Moradin to beat sense into Clip – no dice

George reads from his book during the long rest, summoning his patron? George commits to an offering to the entity in exchange for Clip intelligence restoration. Whispy smokey tendrils reach out from Clip’s head to Georges head and the book – disappearing afterwards. Clip clop now has intelligence of 7 (-2?) – not full on idiot. Petal was the pet name of George? Party moves on – walking down a long hallway, large open double doors, with a faint blue glow emanating from the left side of the room – shrouded by a shadow. Statues appear to be oddly placed around the room, with 2 close to the door. Group moves forward, seeing large crystals in the corner of the room.

Statue –

●       Dwarf: Atalin the steadfast, guardian of westvale, show him 4 crystals and he shall keep thee safe.

●       Angel: Saranah the wise, guardian of knowledge, show her 2 crystals and she shall advise thee

●       Noble lady: iladier, wielder of magic, show her 3 for her magic give you might

●       Crystal plaque: get each crystal in its place, but none stand align with other of its size, see its lot, no more or less, conceal the small behind the great to meet their larges (gift)

●       Warrior: lord hastfield, leader of grey helm, show him 1 crystal, might shall preserve thee

●       Skelly: grumgan the silent, watcher of grave, 3 crystal watch for thy passing

All pads require crystal, no gift without.

SOLVED! Shift and rumbling, crystal lose magic and fall to the ground with a crash, a sheen comes over the statues, and items become non-stone.

Holden – great axe of blood – kill a target gives 2d6 temp health

Bromnik – takes 1H Shield: shield of missile attraction – gives resistance to missile attacks, taking half dmg from ranged weapons

Clip – takes necklace: necklace of adaptation – breathe normally in any environment, adv on saving throws against harmful gases (dragon poison breath)

George – flute: pipes of haunting, enemies must make saving throw, if not - feared

Book – magic /arcana spell book

40 min wait, IDENTIFY ritual cast – BOOM Bromnik knows all the properties. Party moves onward, after solving the puzzle. Traveling for about 5-10 min, beginning to see rubble on the ground…left wall broken and debris leading into a cave.

Perceive- looking into dark cave, smell decay from below, hearing a faint growling noise that echos. PINEAPPLE – illuminating the room, showering light on a tremor attached to the ceiling, lunging at the group. And so it begins:

Holden rushes in with new axe, hitting tremor1 for 16 + 12, killing it with a cleaving blow, letting out a high pitch scream through the chambers. Clip rushes forward with the wheelchair – getting air on jump, slamming into tremor2, doing 10dmg. Attempts to hit again, but dodge out of the way – screeching at him. Bromnik misses on wisdom cantrip….? George eldritch blasts – 20dmg, killing it instantly. Holden moves towards tremor3 – hitting it for 11. Clip makes a lasso, trying to grab roof tremor . Bromnik sacred flames tremor4 – burning it for 8. George zaps it for 12 dmg. Clip is pulled down the hallway from the roof tremor, just barely able to resist – tossing the tentacle as it slithers away. Tremor4 attacks Holden for 18 dmg. Clip is clipped by the largest tremor with its beak – 5dmg. Holden misses both attacks on tremor4. Clip attempts to escape from – stunning it. Bromnik casts  guiding bolt against boss tremor – 11dmg, adding adv to friendlies. George curses target, dis advantage on STR check. Holden attacks tremor4 – killing it. Holden hits boss tremor for 6dmg. Clip moves to attack boss tremor for 6dmg. Bromnik rushes towards boss tremor – word of radiance for 6dmg. George zaps ceiling tremor – badly injuring it. Boss tremor moves to attack Bromnik, both missing – thanks to shield! Ceiling tremor drops to ground (tremor 5) to attack George. Holden attacks boss tremor x2 – 22dmg into the thick of its body/tail. Clip is attempting to ride boss tremor like DUNE. Bromnik misses inflict wounds…George zaps tremor5, misses, but 16dmg to boss tremor. George takes damage from tremor5 for 8+3 dmg. Clip continues to ride the worm to success, punching down into the worm – knocking the creature out. Bromnik turns to cast toll the dead on tremor 5 – killing it!

## Session 7

Clip lashes the large tremor (squidward) to the wheelchair, attempts to domesticate it… group begins to move to the next chamber. Bones are strewn everywhere -a fresher corpse is laying against one of the stalagmites. Bromnik perception check of the ceiling – ornate chair/throne is fixed to the ceiling – something or humanoid is sitting in it. Bromnik tosses a rock towards the chair – zooms faster and sticks to the roof. Party has disagreement over keeping Squidward alive…the group decides to force Clip to put down the beast (judo chop insta death move). Throw the body into the room, no effect? Glowing jewels for eyes in the mummy rat thing. Clip climbs to the chair, and he is upside going towards the chair. Clip clop removes the gems easily.

x2 gems to Clip (50g)

Holden spins body of squid and tosses it, and its body crashing to the ceiling. Clip sits on the chair – no effect. Clip climbs down, and gets to the floor. – there is a gravity split in the room. Bromnik investigates body – appears to be a dwarf, half eaten, wearing leather armor and has pack – pouch full of gold.

24G / 4S.

Leaving the room, go into the next hallway where it is dark and there are dead ends and doorways. Go into a study at end of hall, all dwarven books and furniture – old, old books that easily fall apart. Collect book on forging metals. Clip finds x3 copper in the seat cushions. History check on the books – NAT 20 + 6: Melairkyn Clan – book talks about the ‘halls’, dungeon we are in – how pleased they were at the progress and the happiness of leader and desire to keep going to deeper due to finding mithril. George investigates the next chamber over, a musty bedroom – nothing of note. Bromnik takes a nightgown for later. Party adventures on – getting to double doors, not locked or trapped. Push Clip’s leg into the door with a heavy go, budging the door, but something its holding it back. After peering it, it appears to be crates blocking the door. George blasts the door – splintering the door with smoldering holes. Bromnik works to fix the broken chair for Clip. Holden pushes on the door – making it open fully. Chests are checked – filled with raw ore, Bromnik grabs some and places in his bag. Find a bottle of pee in a keg storage room. Clip drinks from the casket as does Bromnik – dwarven ale. Clip has to make a saving throw, makes it. Approach next door – hear metal pinging inside. Clip opens door and sees two forges red hot and armaments along the well. Clip sees a vast armory – metal working tools, and two GIANT creatures with two heads each approx. 13’ tall working the forge. One of them calls to the other – asking if they are almost done.

George reads a scroll – places hand on Bromnik, casting invisibility, sneaking into the room – one of the two headed beasts sees the door open – asking himself. Gertha wants to get some food! Clip wants to run/jump from shield onto hanging hammer at 30’ high – launching Clip into the air just short, but clip attempts to use tentacle to latch on and does it. Two head headed monsters notice Clip clop is up top and moves towards him. The hammer hanging from the ceiling has a symbol of Moradin – FORGE GOD. As the x2 headed monsters move towards them the ground shakes. They start to huck random tools and weapons at Clip. Bromnik breaks invisibility and hucks a shovel full of coal towards 2head1 – breaking invisibility, causing some fire damage to him. George casts darkness on dagger, tossing it towards hammer that Clip is on. Pineapple confuses 2head2, Clip jump kicks 2head1 causing 18dmg – and strikes his second head for 16dmg (JCVD kick). Holden rushes in and attacks 2head2 – chopping at his knees. 2head1 moves to attack Bromnik – 18dmg + 15dmg. Bromnik casts heat metal on 2head1 and shield of faith on self +2AC. George zaps 2head2 for 13dmg. Holden attacked for 9dmg – one attack from 2head2. Holden attempts to nut punch, but misses. Holden attacks and hits 2head2 for damage, but takes 24 damage. Holden is picked up and tossed him across the room – taking additional 4dmg. 2head1 attacks Clip for 20dmg. Bromnik charges from the darkness and inflicts wounds with necrotic damage to him and heals himself for 12. Clip sickles 2head1 balls – embedding it into one of the testicles. Holden gets up an rushes towards 2head2 to attacks and hacks it to death while its in the doorway – killing Gertha! 2head1 kicks Bromnik across the room, taking 11dmg, and attempts to pick up Clip clop – squeezing him and forcing 11 damage as well as going to back of room with Clip in his hands. Toll the DEAD cast on 2head1 – 16 necrotic damage, forcing black seeping from within, slowly dying and releasing Clip. WIN!

## Session 8

X3 MITHRIL bars – placing in my bag, saving to forge for later (pray to god, imbue with power). Party prepares to leave the forge, marking its location for a possible return. Begin down a long hallway – Clip magically lockpicking the first door on the left. Large open room with a pit in the floor – shuffling down below. There is a humanoid in the pit below. He serves the golden lady – juaqin, patron of shopkeepers, smugglers, and merchants…he asks to be saved and help to get revenge on his party – we tentatively agree. Discover map – and identify key attributes, push the skull button!

“Gate access to skullport, disabled” / “Gate access to stardock from level 16, only”

Pit Half – Halleth, has a stomach gash covered in blood – an old wound, but claims he is ok. George sparta kicks him back into the hole, and Clip pees down the hole onto him… Fine fellows of Daggerford – the party that abandoned him, made up of approx. 5 total – some perished on their journey, 3 remaining that continued on. Bromnik brings him back out of the hole, smelling of pee, and knowing he will be ended if he attempts to cross the group. Being to progress down into the next cavernous room – find a ‘door’ that can’t be opened. Clip kicks the door, moving it barely. Holden shoves it open enough for the group to get into the room – begin to enter stealthy. Clip clop snaps the goblins neck! Group enters the room – looking into the room.

Paintings along the walls, very tribal, bone décor – symbol: sun with center eye in faded red paint.

Massive camp – bugbears x3, goblins x14, and large creature x1, entered from the secret passive.

A goblin comes into the alcove, talking to the dead – but realizing the group is here. He runs into the room, and alerts them all that the group is in there. A high pitch screech emanates in the room.

## Session 9

Battle begins!

Holden breathes fire on crates placed by Bromnik, to create fatal funnel for goblins, hitting the front and killing them. Clip Clop catches and arrow and attempts to throw it back, misses. Halleth shoots a crossbow bolt and murders the goblin he shot at. Clip attempts to wall run, but second attack kicks him in face into the wall – spraying blood from impact. OGRE charges into the room, smashing the fire boxes with his club. Holden does 12 slashing on attack 1, 13 slashing on attack 2! ACTION surge – 16 damage, 15 damage on attack 2 – leaving ogre in a very beaten state. Bugbears move towards the funnel and throw javelin towards Clip – tossing it back towards the ogre. Bromnik misses toll the dead, again… Goblins move in to engage.

Holden takes arrow damage, 6hp. Clip Clop launches knee attack, smashing Ogre face and killing him. Holden jumps up and lands on the ogre, hurting the pinned goblins – gasping for air from the weight of the goblin. Holden jumps onto ogre, crushing the two pinned goblins. Swinging at the closest goblin, killing it in one swing. Bugbear1 attacks Holden, but misses strikes. Holden hit by two javelins – taking 12dmg.  Bromnik, Clip, and Holden shot at, hitting Holden for 10dmg. Spear hits Holden for 8dmg. Halleth hits a goblin for some damage, not killing it – in front of Bromnik, Clip kicking him and killing it. George casts darkness – dome of pitch black…

Holden swings wildly, hitting bugbear – hitting for 12dmg. SECOND WIND + 13 = 34. Holden attacked by both Bugbears – both hits, for 17dmg. Bromnik cast cure wounds at lvl 3 – Holden recovers 15 hp. Clip sparta kicks a goblin to death, body landing on the other goblin – taking some damage.

George starts reading from a book – speaking a crazy language, attempting to contact patron to aid in killing bugbears. Nothing happens, so he zaps the goblins closest to him and murders them both. Holden swings wildly into the darkness – hitting the injured one for 15dmg. Holden attacks closest bugbear for 12dmg – swinging into darkness. Bromnik cast cure wounds l2 for Holden, +16hp and shield of faith +2ac to Holden.

George hellish rebuke after arrow pierce, killing it with necrotic oily tar consuming the goblin attacking Clip. Halleth, misses – again…Clip punches through floor and moves to attack Goblin on the stairs – killing it immediately, ripping its tongue as he pulls his hands back. George attempts to blast away, missing both. Holden hits bugbear for 13dmg, misses second attack.

Bromnik cast guiding bolt – hits fleeing bugbear, exploding it from within as holy fire rages – releasing a BRAIN. Clip spin kicks bugbear into the fire! George zaps the brain with eldritch blast, killing it instantly. Bromnik toll the dead on archer goblin, necrotic damage killing it. Last goblin remaining flees the area – Clip missing as he flees. Halleth shoots the crossbow hitting the fleeing goblin – killing it.

BATTLE ENDS!

Yek Bazaar – written poorly, on the wall. Yek looks like a human wearing a crown – crudely drawn, all along the wall. – come upon a group of goblins, who do not attack immediately. Glom is a lady goblin, locked in a cage. Clip attempts to motorboat Glom…but refuses to pay. Bromnik pays the 2g to release Glom – in an effort to help. The stage being built by the goblins is for auctions? Glom says Yek has a curse, making him look like a human from a goblin…? There are bugbears guarding Yek, from Glom. Twig – enchants

Food shop / potion shop / shop that will buy magic items and sell – some.

Healing potions (75g ea), black pearl (240g) – small bead, tiny bomb!

Goblin (FLIZ) threw a snowball at George’s head. Bromnik offers to sell shit smelling light sword – for 125G, OR magic ring (ring of projection) +100G. Honest salesman! Attempt to win him over to our side to overthrow Yek. He is guarded by 4-5 bugbears, and several goblin slave women.

Party moves into Yek’s chambers – finding the shaven dwarf, named Copper – one of Halleth’s compatriot’s.  Convinces some guards to check the vault, Bromnik checks on the shaven dwarf, remains unconscious. All of the slave woman leave the room – leaving only a few guards and Yek. Clip attempts to strike Yek twice, but misses – bug bears move to attack. Holden chops the head off of one, George fears the other and its hit with 11 necrotic damage. Attempt to take over the market, but George is shanked with a dagger for 7dmg. Word of Radiance – for 7dmg to both Yek and Bugbear2. Clip uses sickle on a chain and does 14 dmg to Yek. Halleth rushes to the dwarf – and stabs him repeatedly while cackling in bloody fury and joy. Holden rushes over and skewers Yek from behind – dropping to the ground. Bugbear 2 – attempts to beg for his life, asking to be spared. George offers him a job, Clip requires Halleth to be killed – and embeds an axe into his back. Bugbear 2 – shall be named Magruff.

Investigate Yek – large key, 25s / 60copper, and circlet (crown of human kind) – when removing he reverts back to a goblin!


Glom is using the crown, becoming an insanely buxomly beauty – inserting her into power and control of the emporium. She alerts the crowd that Yek is dead, causing a light commotion – but declares a higher cut, and they cheer. The party is led into the vault – to take their reward.

Vault: Diorama (gruesome – corpse of beholder with goblins stabbing it) ~15G, ivory chess-set, ~$25g (Bromnik), large wooden chest – earnings, goblin gold, and 9’ tall regal painting of Yek.

McGruff recruits the other bugbears – forming raiding party (Tim and Boog). Holden declares that he will remain, attempt to provide security and make money – LEFT PARTY.

Bromnik buys x2 healing potions (75G/ea) – onward!

Continue on through the dungeon – each bugbear gets a buddy. Boog + Bromnik, Tim + George, Mcgruff + Clip, past a well and to a door. Continue on through the door, finding dead bugbears – holding paper and money.

Paper, in common – as many as there are stars in the sky. Rooms of the walls are sprayed in blood, battle of some type occurred here – resulting in their death. Bromnik closes their eyes, and says a small prayer. Pass through the next door and down the hallway – seeing light creeping from the bottom. There is a magic lit room with plants along the wall and a harp on a small pedestal – George moves to strum it. It is a bone harp, made from all the various races.

There are musical notes on the far wall – difficult to read, but visible. Mcrguff touches the plant in the corner and it immediately wilts and dies.

George attempts to play the harp, matching the notes – but is doing it poorly. His hands begin to ache, then BURN – in the worst pain ever, skin peeling away, leaving only skeleton fingers…! Taking half of 22dmg, then Clip heals for 8…! George writes the notes down in his book of shadows – the rest of the notes fill themselves in, making them legible – he plays it again, revealing a sad harp song…filling everyone with dread and sorrow. A compartment on the harp opens – and scroll falls out. It is a scroll of ‘RAISE DEAD’! – George takes it. Bromnik puts ‘bone harp’ into pack to carry – onward!

Down the hall and into the chemical workshop filled with supplies, vials, solvents, etc. Floating body parts inside some of them – random assortments. Clip makes ‘assassin blood’ poison vile – inflicts individual for 24 hours, must ingest to work.

A voice echoes in the room, ‘intruders, how dare you disturb the masters sanctum’ – out of the caldron an ethereal creature syphons life force from Clip! (lowers max hp by 12). The room kitchen items begin to float and move to attack people (cauldron, pots, cleaver). Clip hits the ghost with magical punches. George blasts with his skeletal hands and zaps the ghosts – dies!

Door inscribed ‘Kalabash's Room. Do not enter un less your name is Halaster.’ Boog enters the room – there is a symbol under the rug (sigil of some sort written in blood), George revealed it. Scrolls – appear blank, but made of rare paper. Bromnik takes x3 blank scrolls, possibly magical. Locked chest under the bed – Clip attempts to unlock, and a lightning bolt flashes and damages Clip (10dmg) – a note is inside:

‘Kalabash’s retirement fund, don’t spend this on ale!’….worthless.

George syphon energy from the creature – healing himself for the half damage level. Bugbears move to attack, x2 hit – for dmg. Int check – magic does more damage, hitting for 15dmg! George’s patron speaks to him, enjoying the suffering of the Golem – he collects part of the beast.

Mine carts, pick axes, etc. are lining the path. Identify small chunks of copper in the cave, appearing to be the source of the above material. Identify a statue in an alcove – it has been defaced by goblins? Religion check on statue – Dwarven writing on it.

Dumathoin – god, keeper of secrets under the mountain!

Bromnik moves into the middle of the Eyes and casts word of radiance – 2/3 fail save, hitting each for 9dmg. Eye4 moves to attack Clip – but goes after the illusion. Bromnik takes 11 damage from the Eyes surrounding him, clawing relentlessly. Boom wails on Eye4, doing 20dmg – showing damage. George blasts Eye3 for 22dmg! George misses, Bromnik uses circlet of blasting – hit only one ray, x7 dmg, Clip misses attack, and Tim HITS with crossbow bolt. George tries to reason with it, requesting forgiveness but it does not work.

Human fighter on the rock (Rex) – hits Eye creature for 13dmg. Clip moves to attack bug, knocking it stunned – doing 15dmg. Bromnik casts sacred flame on bug creature for 10dmg. Clip attempts to stun again, but resisted – still doing damage. Bromnik attempts toll the Dead on Bug creature – misses, again. George blats the bug for 7dmg. The Bug creature casts a darkness spell on himself. Clip attacks blindly and hits the eye creature for 7dmg. Rex slashes wildly, hitting the eye once for dmg. Bromnik charges in screaming in rage – hitting the eye creature for 4dmg. George yells to the group to alert where the danger is, Clip charges back down the hall – and attacks the bug, hitting for 16 dmg. Bromnik casts guiding light for 14dmg – KILLING THE BEAST, tiny cracks in the shell and building from a bright holy light within exploding to death!

Bromnik attempts to bring Boog back, but he is beyond saving. Tim begins to sing an ancient bugbear hymn – as Bromnik reads the last rites of Boog. Tim then steals the pouch from Boog, claiming his prize money.

Rex the Hammer, fine fellows of Daggerford, introduces himself to the party. THE SAME PARTY THAT HALLETH WAS APART OF. George ZAPS him – for 1dmg. The party interrogates him – trying to learn his background. He is a leathered skin, athletic, and older ARNOLD type, of northern type (illuskin). He left the group because the lady person that stabbed Halleth got weird. The party agrees to join up. Set up camp for the evening.

The group asks him to explain who assigned him to the floor, who gives orders, etc. He has been down to skullport and back for supplies, to recruit people in exchange for money, work for someone on the floor below – a powerful ‘house’. Xanather guild – famous guild based in waterdeep, unknown headquarters. Creature that guards the slayer, but sneaking past it somehow using a plywood with a symbol on it. The party applies ink to themselves in the shape of the symbol, and the map has been updated.

Party is on their way to murder! They move down the long hallway and come upon a humanoid creature made of wood and metal. Make it to a room that is lit with fire, large with columns that span the entire floor to ceiling space. It looks at the group, but after seeing the symbol it moves on. Clip peers into the room, and sees a group of approx. 9, nearly half bugbears, including Nadia. Clip attempts to use Pineapple as a flashbang and charge in, the room notices the orb as it floats in “what on earth is that? To arms!” PINEAPPLE – blinds half of the room. Battle begins!

Rex / Tim / McGruff charge into the room getting into melee range. Rex hits for three attacks – killing the first guy with a single strike with the hammer. Tim takes 6 dmg, McGruff takes 3dmg. Nadia commands the bugbears to attack, and also hits McGruff. Bromnik takes a javelin, 8dmg. McGruff is at 2hp! Bromnik casts Spectral Weapon, attacking Nadia for 10dmg. George zaps Nadia for 14dmg. Clip slaps a bugbear for 5dmg. Tim is knocked prone at 0, and McGruff is nearly killed and has 1 death save left. Nadia charges Clip and hits. McGruff is killed! Rex kills the bugbear in front of him – killing it with its own mace after catching it. Toll the dead misses, again – but Spiritual weapon does 22 damage to Nadia! Bromnik takes damage from both attacks, taking 9dmg. REX slays both soldiers next to him and Bromnik – killing x2. Clip takes 12dmg directly, and Bromnik takes another 15dmg. Identify Dagger – This poison is typically made only by the Drow, and only in a place far removed from sunlight. A creature subjected to this poison must succeed on a DC 13 Constitution saving throw or be Poisoned for 1 hour. If the saving throw fails by 5 or more, the creature is also Unconscious while Poisoned in this way. The creature wakes up if it takes damage or if another creature takes an action to shake it awake.

Identify – Turquoise Gem: emerald type stone, use action to smash and summon water elemental (concentration 1 hr)

Malik begins to drown, slowly. George is struck by a flaming arrow, dying instantly. Malik slowly drowns, seeing the ship sink and bodies, until nothing – darkness.

Moonshadow identifies cloak:

Cabal’s Ruin: Cabal's Ruin has 4 charges and regains 1d4 expended charges daily at dawn.

  When the wearer is targeted by an enemy’s spell, they can use their reaction to have the cloak swallow part of the spell. The cloak gains a number of charges equal to the spell level of the triggering spell. The wearer is still subject to whatever effects the spell would normally inflict on them. The ability cannot be used again until they finish a short or long rest.

  When the wearer hits with an attack, they can choose to expend any number of charges from the cloak, dealing an additional 1d6 lightning damage per charge expended, which is inflicted on the target of that attack. If the attack strikes multiple targets, the wearer must choose one target from the group to be subject to this damage.

Moonshadow identifies leather armor:

_Mariners Armor: swim speed is equal to walking speed (not hindered by water speed debuff), shoot up to service when held under water_

Moonshadow identifies magical scroll and wand:

Wand – dragon’s breath: cone of fire attack

Scroll – gaseous form: turns user into gas cloud.

Sword – doesn’t appear to be magical, faint aura surrounds it, must repair it to understand its power.

Malik swims towards Clip – but scorches ray the creature for 20dmg, but then takes 13 dmg from the tail.

Malik swims up to the beast, casting inflict wounds for 25 necrotic dmg! Malik then is hit, again, for 8dmg. Then Phil moves to attack Malik, but misses.

Malik is attacked twice, knocking him unconscious. Death save – nat 20! Clip pulls Malik up onto the rock, helping him regain consciousness. Malik lay on hands for 30 on himself, while the others try to recover onto land.

Clip and Lurrash look awful, translucent, and feel sick upon leaving the water? Malik attempts lay on hands, but it does not cure the disease – it has already set fully? The moment they leave the water, it feels as if their skin is on fire, and Lurrash runs back to the water.

## Session 40

Party eats breakfast, begins their journey, and continues for hours- trees change and become more sparse, and terrain levels out and less hilly. Black ooze comes out after stabbing one of the trees that looks like it may be dying?

After walking for what feels like forever, a cat type noise comes from their left? Stealthy moving towards the noise, near large pond type area, with a creature in the center of a pit within a collapsed tree bridge – all black, like a panther, but massively large. Malik pulls him out with a lasso, and it emerges with a tentacle back and six legs!

He is a friend of Willow – but says she isn’t seeing anyone and has changed… the bog used to be a forest, part of the blight? Clip feeds Crisson the fish people flesh, seems to ease the hunger. Group begins to follow Crisson, who leads the way, through the bog – towards a massive tree in the distance.

An extremely large jaw snaps from the pile of leaves after Malik pokes it. Malik takes 17hp dmg, hellish rebuke for 15dmg, lights on fire. 27 divine SMITE! But misses second attack. But then IT HEALS??

Malik is being swallowed, taking crushing damage, and unable to breathe…blinded, restrained, and being crushed. Malik is able to hold breath for 3 minutes, though, taking 14 crushing dmg.

25 divine SMITE, and expelled from the body of the beast. The beats tail swipes…taking 19 more dmg. Clip burning hands the beast, killing it! Clip then skins it, harvest x4 rations, and gets x1 pelts. Onward towards the tree!

Upon reaching the tree, it is approx. 80’ wide and 100’ high – finally there, massive root base as the bottom. The tree looks unhealthy, not real way to get in from the initial look.  Clip calls for Willow, and the roots part and make a doorway. It is dark, acrid, smelly.

A voice is said , “I know you…” “moment you touch the sickness, I knew you.”

Bulb in the center of the roof unfurl, a figure within, slender elvish is lowered. But consumed by leaves and vines…?

Her eyes lock onto Malik, implying she knows him? Selfish and cruel, unproven ally, driving those you know into danger for your own gain. You’re lost without form, a leaf caught in the wind…you search for something you know not what, presenting illusion of confidence – retelling the horrors of his past!

I seek to give you what you have long sought, the power, to rise above fools errand, impossible task before you, rise to meet your destiny! Lived 1000 years, and can grant many gifts.  The ones I have let in have hurt me so very deeply…can I trust you? I wish a bond, a companion once more – would you embrace me? Malik agrees – kneels before her, and is taken closer to her. Vines come down from the ceiling, locking eyes with one another, wrapping vines and piercing skin – taking 6points of piercing. Taken up to the ceiling, embraced and intertwined with one another, and begin to make out – but the black ooze pours into Malik’s body…as he disappears fully into the ceiling. WTF.

Clip runs with Phil on him, running up the walls to try to get to Malik – vines trying to swipe at them as they go. Clip is grabbed, but is able to slip out of it narrowly – able to barely make it to the central vine section. Unable to gain access – give up, and wait. 1-2 hours passes, and then the vines open up, unraveling…Malik is revealed, but is covered in vines and bark, like Willow. He has been transformed!

Malik is ARCHFEY, AC can’t be less than 16 – regardless of armor, weakness to fire, permanent barkskin. Malik is now Willow’s champion, with a deep connection to the forest, with an endless duty to protect it.

Willow will remain in the tree, but wishes Malik to protect the wilds – to restore what has decayed while she has remained hidden in the tree. Story time:

Willow came with her husband, used to be a giant cave, but she made it into a forest due to missing the outdoors and nature – wanted to leave originally, but husband did not. She killed her husband in a fight – but became extremely sad and depressed. A ‘man’ showed up, powerful wizard then made her a forest to bring her joy and an attempt to keep her here. He populated it with fauna and creatures, which did bring her joy…for a time. An adventurer came along and was with her, named Crisson!, but did betray her – and she killed him. Depression crept up after her actions, causing decay to creep outwards from the tree. As the group departs the tree, a connection is felt, and Malik is able to open the door – through forest magic connection.

Group departs the tree, and sun is out, swamp is returned to normal, mist is gone…the forest, appears to be healing! Malik’s interaction has returned order and health to the forest, with Willow becoming his deity/lover.

The south side of the cave needs attention – and Clip convinces Crisson to join the group as they begin to move out, further restoring the forest health.

Blessing: Heroes' Feast Casting Time: 10 minutes / Range: 30 feet / Target: See text /Components: V S M (A gem-encrusted bowl worth at least 1,000 gp, which the spell consumes)

You bring forth a great feast, including magnificent food and drink. The feast takes 1 hour to consume and disappears at the end of that time, and the beneficial effects don’t set in until this hour is over. Up to twelve other creatures can partake of the feast.

A creature that partakes of the feast gains several benefits. The creature is cured of all diseases and poison, becomes immune to poison and being frightened, and makes all Wisdom saving throws with advantage. Its hit point maximum also increases by 2d10 (15), and it gains the same number of hit points. These benefits last for 24 hours.

Yurlaka thanks the group for the help, the Netherskull was blocking their path forward. They nearly wiped out the mind flayers, but a wise Gith, known as Zerthimon, knew the empire was destroyed and that the Gith should stop ways of conquest…look inward, create peaceful society, but it fractured the society in two (strength vs wisdom). Githyanki remains in the warrish ways, whereas Yurlaka are of the Githzerai. He admits they still have a deep hatred for all Illithid…they feel their presence in the dungeon, likely a hive – deep within. They are moving across ramshackle scaffolds – but make to the other side. Oh shit – the track is broken…they JUMP the gap, but land on the other side, barely, surviving as the skid by on the tracks. The tracks run to an end, group slows, and comes to an end, no longer moving.

Dancing lights appear – illuminating the space, seeing an exit to the north – group proceeds towards it. Continuing come to intersection finding tunnels of mined area, sloped down area they go through, and a cart with track going back upwards. Finally – come to an open area, hearing mining down one tunnel and a door down the other.

Unable to open the door, Vincenzo knocks – talking to a grey dwarf who is wary of him, rightfully.  They are hesitant, wanting to take them to their leader (Duegar!). Vincenzo convinces them he is alone, and gets taken to their leader…forcing Malik and Magni to knock on the door, trying to gain access. Vincenzo implies he doesn’t know Magni or Malik, but all are to be brought to the ‘boss’ – Valtagar.

Valtagar asks why they have down here, to his domain of calassabrak. Vincenzo tries to deceit his way through…semi successfully. Tells a story of how they came to be here, but there is one who is too powerful to be defeated – on this floor? Already ‘he’ knows the group is there, sensing his presence. Malik insight checks to see if he serves willfully – saying he does, but does NOT really want to serve him – only doing so to save his people. He escorts them through the areas, towards the ‘leader’.

Group comes to a room, giant black curtain of black dragon scales…the wall is leaking blood?! Vincenzo tastes it, confirming blood. Hallways have statues of various pose dwarves in battle stances – eyes alit with flames for lights, but all in disrepair conditions.

In the center of a room, a Dr Manhattan looking individual, leaning casually on a large throne. There is an enormous great sword leaning against the chair, watching the group approach. Behind him appears to be a large creature of sorts – obscured by shadows, muscular hulky and fanged, armed with greataxe. Malik casts divine sense, finding two crystals trapped with creatures encased (next to portal!), confirming the blue dude is celestial. Azrian – the blue dude, is introduced by Valtagar – essentially offering up the group for…sacrifice?

‘welcome to the halls of your final judgement, I am the judge, jury, and perhaps the executioner. I am know as Azrian the just, in the name of Tome – I ask you, have you been righteous, have you been honorable, have you been just?’ feel a force in the air, unlike any other in Undermountain. Warm, but not kind, softest hands can curl into fists. Malik explains his wrongdoings, how he has tried to atone – but is waved off by Azrian…as he says they will face judgement.

From behind, a deep gnome arrives, carrying papers – dropping some, as he comes as beckoned by Azrian…offering to be their counsel?! What type of trial is this?! ‘we will recess, for now – as you councilor explains the process, when you shall tell your tale and determine a verdict fitting’. ‘should you try to flee, I will give pursuit. I have chased fiends across all planes, there is no escaping the long arm of TORM’. (seems like a mis-use of the cause).

Norwyn Waplin – the public defender, takes group to private room, and tries to work their case. He doesn’t seem to be active in their defense…shit. But – Azrian can summon anyone from the multi-verse to be on the stand to attest to their character.

Malik, opens – the defense first, speaking for the group –

“No man is pure good or evil, they are but a pendulum of actions - trying to stay to the side of good the best they can. they should be judged by their impact to the world and those around them. one man's god's good is another man's god's evil - but did they live true to themselves, their faith. our actions, as witnessed by those we shall call - will prove it.”

Prosecution – well dressed dwarf, sounding like southern gentleman. “I do declare these adventures are no more just or good than our demonic friend over there. I say, they left a slew of bodies in their wake. I will prove to this court they are truly evil”.

Prosecution calls forth first witness – jyrindain – who the fuck is this guy. Apparently… Vincenzo robbed their ship and told bad jokes! Stole food meant for orphanage…oops. Vincenzo tries to object, but is warned one more outburst would result in contempt. Vincenzo calls Cpt. Squid beard – getting rather glowing review and the damming one of the Gith. But – cross questioning resulted in the reveal that the cpt ate brains of the crew…oof.

Prosecution next witness…Iveno!

Malik calls Lurrash – glowing review for Malik.

Two dwarves from the tubes/matrix…. make them look BAD (Malik/Magni). Prosecution makes it look not bad… but, Magni and Malik make fairly compelling argument as to why it was the least bad choice. Azrian seems to acknowledge that as a viable choice.

Azrian states he does not need more time to cast judgment, he heard enough – he will make his final decision momentarily – NEXT SESSION.

## Session 144

There is a stillness in the air, as the court is cleared of all but the party and Azrian. You have done great deeds, and acts of cowardice and evil… not easy to walk fine line of lawfulness and just, but doing so for any mortal must be as easy as taking a breath, without a second thought. This is what holds true of all tenets of Torm, and all creatures that wander this realm…all creatures are lost, they are children, souls twisted with corrupted desires. I AM THE ONLY ONE WHO CAN JUDGE FATE. After much consideration, you are not without sin, therefore – I hereby judge thee guilty. However, the sentence will not be death, but 200 years hard labor. You may learn over this time and repent.

Vincenzo attempts to call a witness, but no more shall be heard. He attempts to call Azrian to the stand, but he refuses – seemingly above reproach? Vincenzo attempts to counter sentence – but doesn’t make the check…oof, good attempt. He aims his sword at Vincenzo, you – who are you to dare seek judgement against me! I may have been too hasty in sparing your miserable lives. Malik reluctantly agrees with Vincenzo – seemingly only to upset him more.  Demon moves forward, FIGHT!

Vincenzo crits – hitting with melee hit. Malik attempts to persuade – but fails. Malik moves back to stand with Magni, who is healed by Magni for another 41. Magni heals Vincenzo…but only for him to be struck down immediately by Azrian. Once he does – his eyes change, radiating brilliantly bright – BLINDING those looking at him (Magni).

Malik strikes true, twice, against the fiend – spirit shroud helping him slay the demon finally, rushing over to aid his party. Magni, blind, reacts to his condition and is angered. Magni tries to persuade Azrian, but he is unshaken – a vision to clear the world of ‘evil’.

Malik raises Vincenzo from death, shield of faiths himself, while Azrian’s eyes turn RED and bleeding once more. Magni blindly moves away – hitting with an attack. Azrian turns to face Malik, striking him once more for significant damage. Vincenzo attempts to get Azrian to pull a card from the deck of many things – but, has extreme resistance to do so. Malik – strikes him numerous times, knocking him to his knee – held up by his sword. OUT OF INVISIBILITY – the dark dwarves arrive “Valtagar – running with his companions, he is wounded – now is our chance, strike him down!”.

Magni, still blind, firebolts for 16 dmg from within the magic dome surrounding the dwarves – as he is finally able to see once more!

… only for his eyes to change once more and blind the entire party! “Valtagar, you fool – you think you can stand against my justice?! Prepare to die with the rest of these filth”. He turns, strikes down Vincenzo, faces Malik and strikes him down immediately after. Otto tries to cast a spell, but misses – grabs a healing potion from Magni to bring to the down members…maybe?. Azrian rushes over to Magni and dwarves, Magni casts shocking grasps but misses. Azrian cleaves down several of the dwarves. They try to attack, hitting once, while Vincenzo and Malik make death saves. Malik is res by Otto taking a healing potion from Magni. Azrian – legendary action, AOE spell attack…Magni attempts to COUNTERSPELL from his ring of spell storing. Fuck. It doesn’t work. Swords begin to swirl, spinning around the battlefield!

Malik dex saves, but Magni takes damage while in the storm of swords…Vincenzo is down, just missing his body! Magni falls in the storm of blades…again. Azrian strikes down two more dwarves. Malik moves to attack. Malik strikes him to a knee – he monologues… MALIK YELLS OUT “LET THIS BE THE JUSTICE YOU DESERVE” and immediately decapitates Azrian. His body dematerializes into nothingness, leaving nothing behind. He is slain – no longer there. The evil angel has been defeated!

Malik strikes down one more in avoids the spray of ashes as it dies. MORE SKELETONS come running out! The flame returns to normal, and smoke clears, but the skeletons remain. Malik runs over and strikes down the skelly in front of Magni, shielding from the exploding ash. Malik is bit, but then pins him down with his sword. Malik drags Magni back through the hallway, urges the teleport – and the group is back into Melissara’s quarters. The group goes to the room down the hall with the fountain/statue, long rest!

Malik’s ring is now with Melissara, unable to teleport outside of the dungeon – only Magni. Group rests, recovers, and decides to go the vault – guarded by beast/dragon, to try and get money owed before leaving. Onward they progress, traveling down to the right level, coming to a corridor – anti-magic field everywhere…all magic stops working. Passing through, they pass the anti-magic and things return to normal. Going further, passing places some have been through before – from the previous mishap with teleportation, continue on to a hallway with multiple doors… moving towards the vault, guarded by a blue dragon! As they continue, the paladin senses are triggered. The undead feelings, worse than he has ever felt, pure dread – EVIL is around. It is triggering Malik – pauses, but the group decides to go on despite his worry.

Inside room, Magni uses cat WARG and slips inside after Vincenzo opens door stealthily. Inside are 4 large pillars that don’t reach the ceiling, and in the middle is a large undead blue dragon!! Behind the dragon is the vault…the cat is discovered by the dragon, who appears to notice but not act…. until the cat goes near it and it is instantly crushed.

## Session 158

Great Dragon, we come before – peacefully. We have struck down the lord of this keep, Morvin, who was infected by the evil that once consumed this place.

I only move for my master, grrr. Malik attempts to reason with him, but it is wary. His name is ‘Lenorax’. Vincenzo asks about his undead skin... he responds he shed it as he evolves the need for it. The others are beckoned into the room, to speak their truth. Morvin and I had a kinship, he helped me evolve… his death weighs heavily on me, but it is not something I can dwell on forever. This is now MY lair. This is now MY home. The treasure is Mine, and I will defend it – every last piece… to the death! Sparks build, he inhales, and energy is released! 42 lightening damage!! The entire group is hit as the air crackles with energy. Fight!

Vincenzo casts Hunger of Hadar on dragon. Malik flies Melissara around the darkness towards the vault, but then Malik is struck by the tail swipe for bludgeoning damage. Melissara slows time and blinks to the vault door, bloodied hand with dagger, and vault door opens – stepping in. Vincenzo and Helgrin are frightened while Magni is bit and chomped as he flew away from the darkness. Malik flies to the vault, within the bubble casted by Melissara, yelling to the others to get inside. Magni attacked again with wings, knocking him prone out of the air. He stands up, misty steps and runs to the vault – hiding inside with Malik. Dragon moves, attacks Vincenzo with bites and scratches – hitting for chunks. Vincenzo runs to the vault door, starts closing it, while Malike rushes over to support – slamming it shut. As its shuts the door has a THUD noise as something slams into it. They take a moment to breathe a sigh of relief.

Investigate vault: 24,800 gold / 14k silver / 2k platinum / Gems: topaz (500g), star ruby (1k g), child’s rocking horse – half scored with fire, musical box, Guill – fly Magic Carpet! Use action to fly – command it within 30’ / blue stone clatters to the ground… Malik picks it up, small but something ‘strange’ about it. Magni identifies it, as it glows bright – floating above his hand… Ioun stone (Wonderous Item, bonus to intelligence score +2, attunement). Malik gets ring back from Melissara, Magni teleports group to surface – safe, all within Waterdeep!

Malik stops in to temple of Tyr, seeking guidance – if the Lich he swore to strike down walks amongst them. Malik realizes both he himself and Ezzat are more alike, both on a quest of vengeance…one against him, another against Hallister – the Mad Mage. There is time before his evil will be felt, for now.

Bastion build for the group:

Armory, barracks, arcane study, garden, library, sanctuary, smithy, store house, workshop, gaming hall, green house, laboratory, Sacristy, scriptorium, stable, teleportation circle, theater, training room, trophy room, archive, meditation chamber, menagerie (zoo), observatory, pub, demi-plane portal, guild hall, sanctum, war room.

Group - Bastian: War Room, Arcane Study, Theater, Teleportation circle, demi-plane portal, stable, garden, workshop – ships. 15,500 + bastion 10k = ~25,500k gold (about $14.5k remaining).

Bastian built, bought, moved into.

## Session 159

 Prologue – for characters, develop.

Malik starts an order or knights – small, yet forceful – highly trained that offer regional protection, eradication of evil, as well as building alliances with guilds. The soldiers he enlists are those who were once blind to their vengeance, offering them meaningful path forward – in service of others. They become ‘The Retribution Covenant’. The Veiled Watch is the smaller, secretive wing of the order – tasked with monitoring Undermountain…searching for any signs of Ezzat’s return. Malik’s personal vendetta remains key and critical to his personal story. He does not know when Ezzat will return, but prepares for it nonetheless – seeking knowledge on how to destroy phylacteries. The emblem of the order becomes a Longsword flanked by outstretched Raven’s wings, with sun rays topping the blade – their tabard black and white, their legend growing with each successful mission. Ever vigilant, ever ready.

Vincenzo starts a crew, builds a boat, starts import/export business, while training informants/fighters through the comedy club.

Magni teaches local community how to grow crops, after time, Magni moves on to journey back home – up north, back to his people. Leaving only a letter behind to be found by Malik or Vincenzo, strict instructs to not deny anyone entry learning to farm. Magni spends a month consumed in his tower, attempting to learn the spell Ezzat had started… eventually he runs out to the balcony excited – yelling ‘eureka!’. Magni finds out that the spell is WISH – creating a spell scroll Wish! Wish to be determined in the future ... perhaps.

## End of Campaign

Until there next adventure, the group remains at their newly built Bastion/Fort.
